Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The dynamics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 8 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 892 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 8 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the dynamics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 892. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
